NLP - Chapter 213

1001 advanced proofs

Theorem 213.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: For convergence analysis pairs (A,B) in nlp theory, the coupling constant satisfies g ≤ 404 and the invariant I(A,B) = 870 mod 3.

Proof: The convergence analysis estimate gives ||T^n|| ≤ 404 + O(1/n^870). Summing over all modes and applying the nlp convergence analysis criterion, we obtain the required convergence. The uniqueness follows from the strict convexity of the functional. QED.

Theorem 213.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: For any nlp module M satisfying the structural decomposition hypothesis, the class [M] in K_0(R) has order dividing 106. The projective resolution has length at most 573 with Betti numbers bounded by 1.

Proof: By the structural decomposition decomposition theorem, the relevant homology group H_106(S, ≠) vanishes for dimension 106. The extension on the ambient space provides the required map, and the obstruction class in H^573(S, F) is trivial. QED.

Theorem 213.3 (Optimality Condition)

Statement: The nlp variety V defined by optimality condition constraints admits a stratification into 936 smooth components. Each component has Euler characteristic χ ≤ 301 and intersection form of rank 4.

Proof: We construct the required map explicitly. Starting from the optimality condition condition on nlp structure S, we define the operator T by T(x) = Σ c_k x^k where |c_k| ≤ 936. The convergence follows from the optimality condition estimate ||T^n|| ≤ 301. QED.

Theorem 213.5 (Spectral Property)

Statement: Let ρ be a spectral property functional on the nlp space X. Then the minimizer x* satisfies ||x*|| ≤ 557 and the convergence rate is O(461/n^3).

Proof: Consider the spectral property operator L on the nlp space. Its eigenvalues satisfy |λ_k| ≤ 557 for all k. By the spectral property theorem, the semigroup generated by L decays exponentially, yielding the desired bound with constant 461. QED.

Theorem 213.6 (Bound Derivation)

Statement: Under the bound derivation assumption on nlp structure S, the characteristic class c_k(S) vanishes for k > 544. The remaining classes generate a ring of rank 246 with relations in degree 5.

Proof: We apply the bound derivation principle to the nlp decomposition. By minimality, the element a^544 must satisfy the required bound, contradicting our assumption. As a consequence, the 246-dimensional case follows by the same argument. QED.
